11:20:3511 heures, 20 minutes et 35 secondesThe next way of increasing the number of purchases that your customers make is if you're selling something which is either consumed or needs to be renewed to run
11:20:4411 heures, 20 minutes et 44 secondesmarketing campaigns that are about to enter this crucial renewal or repurchase cycle. One example of a B2B client that
11:20:5311 heures, 20 minutes et 53 secondeswe work with sells an accreditation and this accreditation expires every year or two. So we did some work to help them
11:21:0011 heures et 21 minutesincrease the percentage of their members that renew their subscription. We set up automated email marketing campaigns that
11:21:0711 heures, 21 minutes et 7 secondesactivated just before people were due to renew. This resold them on the benefits of this particular accreditation and encouraged them to take the steps to
11:21:1611 heures, 21 minutes et 16 secondesrenew. But we didn't just leave it there. We also fed the user data of the people that were about to come up for renewal to Google and Facebook in order
11:21:2411 heures, 21 minutes et 24 secondesto run targeted ads to these people both on Google Display Network and through Facebook and Instagram. We also set up
11:21:3211 heures, 21 minutes et 32 secondesan automated workflow that fed the user data of the people that were about to come up for renewal to Google and Facebook so that we could run targeted
11:21:4111 heures, 21 minutes et 41 secondesmarketing to them on Google Display Network, Facebook and Instagram, running display ads to emphasize again the benefits of renewing their subscription.
11:21:5011 heures, 21 minutes et 50 secondesThis means that when people came up for renewal, they started getting emails about renewal and they started seeing ads about renewal. By covering our bases
11:21:5811 heures, 21 minutes et 58 secondesand not relying on any one marketing channel to start this renewal conversation, it means that if somebody for example doesn't see the email or
11:22:0611 heures, 22 minutes et 6 secondesdoesn't see the display ads, we've still got a fallback method of contacting them. So this works well for any business where there is a defined period
11:22:1411 heures, 22 minutes et 14 secondesof membership, i.e. something needs to be renewed or it's a consumable product.
11:22:2011 heures, 22 minutes et 20 secondesLet's say for example that you're selling moisturizer. The first step you would want to take with this is to identify the average repurchase
11:22:2711 heures, 22 minutes et 27 secondesfrequency. Now, if you're selling a year's membership to something, the repurchase is obvious. It's a year. But if you're selling something like a
11:22:3411 heures, 22 minutes et 34 secondesmoisturizer, you would want to know how often people on average repurchase that product. What you then want to do is
11:22:4111 heures, 22 minutes et 41 secondesstart the promotion for the repurchase or renewal before people are due to run out. What you don't want is for that
11:22:4911 heures, 22 minutes et 49 secondesperson to run out of moisturizer, go online and start shopping for new replacement moisturizer. You want a
11:22:5611 heures, 22 minutes et 56 secondeschance to get them before they enter that new customer journey and start shopping. The same with the renewal. You
11:23:0311 heures, 23 minutes et 3 secondeswant to start targeting people before they come up for renewal so that they already have in their mind and can begin selling themselves on the prospect of
11:23:1211 heures, 23 minutes et 12 secondesrenewal. If you're a business that sells something where customers can upgrade, for example, you release a new version, then you can increase the purchase
11:23:2111 heures, 23 minutes et 21 secondesfrequency by running promotions to your existing customers, advising them of the upgrade. This is an Aura ring. It
11:23:2711 heures, 23 minutes et 27 secondesbasically tracks stats and gives me information.
11:23:3311 heures, 23 minutes et 33 secondesI think this is version two of the ring and late last year, Aura came out with version three. Now, they could have just hoped that I saw all the hype around the
11:23:4211 heures, 23 minutes et 42 secondesnew version and decided to upgrade. But what they did instead was they ran a promotion to existing owners of the Aura Ring, explaining what the new version did and incentivizing them to upgrade.
11:23:5411 heures, 23 minutes et 54 secondesThey offered a discount and free lifetime membership to their new subscription, which they added, which is a smart move because it would
11:24:0211 heures, 24 minutes et 2 secondeshave cost them very little to get me to repurchase as opposed to getting someone new who started researching fitness trackers to purchase an Aura Ring for
11:24:1011 heures, 24 minutes et 10 secondesthe first time. They passed some of that saving on to me as an incentive to make another purchase. Now, one of the great
11:24:1811 heures, 24 minutes et 18 secondesthings about this strategy is that when Aura reached out, they didn't need to sell me on the overall benefits of owning an Aura ring. I already understand what it is and what it does.
11:24:2811 heures, 24 minutes et 28 secondes[music] So, they could tailor their marketing specifically to telling me what was new about the new version versus the one I had. Now, if we go back
11:24:3611 heures, 24 minutes et 36 secondesto Teleream and their product ScreenFlow, they do exactly the same thing. When you purchase a ScreenFlow license, every time they upgrade the
11:24:4511 heures, 24 minutes et 45 secondesproduct, you have to buy a new license, but you don't have to go back to the start and pay full price for it. They reach out with an offer to encourage you

11:25:0211 heures, 25 minutes et 2 secondesgot. This makes the marketing much more effective because they're not talking about all the basics of ScreenFlow that I already understand. They're focusing
